  SAFE (named in the job): (a) CDDESIGN Commands/CivilDesignCommand.cs:47 reads via DetentionTwlService.ReadProject -> ProjectDataService.TryPeekCurrent (only this drawing's record, else none) and Save Draft writes via SetJobCouncil/SetJobLandUse (RulebookDrawing.cs:349, LandUse.cs:57), both refuse when the record is not this drawing's. Cost only: in a 2nd drawing CDDESIGN may show "records not loaded" and keep nothing; no swap. (b) Project Review = CDDASHBOARD Commands/DashboardCommands.cs:29 binds (SetDrawingPath) at open, dialog modal (line 91); RecordProjectReviewApproval (Mutations.cs:1324) saves Current but only from inside that bound modal window. (c) CDCHECK Commands/SpineCheckCommand.cs:103 TryPeekCurrent; main-outlet write :417-426 goes to db explicitly. (d) Batch editor (CDEDIT batch) Commands/PipeEditCommand.cs:51 binds at open, BatchEditDialogHost modal, CeBatchEditApplyCoordinator.cs:174-198 writes snapshot to its own database. (e) Pit Schedule export lives in PITSCHEDULE (modal, PitScheduleCommand.cs:30) and is covered by #604's bind in LoadFromProjectData. (f) CDREPAIR Commands/HubCommands.cs:618 is a modal list that only sends command names (CdRepairDialog.xaml.cs:21-27); no record read or write. Also safe: CDCALC (CalculationsCommand.cs:42-47 adopts this drawing's snapshot, modal), CDHUB (reads TryPeekCurrent only, NetworkRedesignCurrentDrawingReader.cs:40), Inspector palette (binds on refresh, InspectorPaletteService.cs:563/1398).
  AT RISK (no bind, then read or write CE's record; with two drawings, or after closing the first, the save goes to the wrong drawing or a closed drawing's record lands in this one): (1) CDSYSTEM Commands/PipeSystemCommand.cs:50-75 reads Current/GetPipeByHandle and UpdatePipeSystemByHandles saves Current. (2) Services/ProjectDataService.cs:576 EnsureDrawingDataCurrent(db) (and SyncWithDrawing :249) sync drawing db's pits/pipes INTO whatever record is in memory: removes the other drawing's pits whose handles are not in db, merges db's in, then saves; reached unbound from CDSLABEDIT SlabCommand.cs:1178, CDSLABEREGEN SlabCommand.cs:1801, CDRAINFALL RainfallCurveCommand.cs:52 (then SetJobRainfallCurve writes Current), CEHEALTH HealthCheckCommand.cs:37.
  Plan: PR A = EnsureDrawingDataCurrent makes the record db's own first (one code path covers (2) and every future caller); PR B = CDSYSTEM binds before it reads. Both stacked on #604 (they reuse its BindToDrawingWithoutSaving).

The flaky TheRecentStripsAndPitScheduleFilters_LeaveCdSettingsByteIdentical does NOT write Tim's real CD_Settings.json: test uses its own temp folder (Bug306RememberedValuesFileTests.cs:34, SettingsIsolationSetup.cs), no parallel tests, live %APPDATA%\TIM_CE_CADTOOLS unchanged across combined runs (only AutoCAD's own files at 00:59 when acad.exe started), CD_Settings.json rewritten 0 times in ~10,000 stressed saves. REAL CAUSE (small product fault, not a test isolation fault): SettingsService.RememberedValues.cs TryWriteRememberedValues writes .tmp then File.Replace; when Windows (scanner/indexer) holds CD_RecentValues.json for a few ms the swap fails once, is only logged, and the file keeps old values (about 1 in 200 in stress, leaves a .tmp). The assertion on the recent file then fails. Not reproduced in 3 combined Services runs (8,879 tests; 3 other reds each time: Glen Eira known, FinalLandingProducerCollapses..., RefusedNativeCallbackInvalidates... probably my placeholder arx); exact exception text not captured.
